<<<BEGIN>>>
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../features/admin/admin_shell.dart';
import '../features/auth/models/auth_models.dart';
import '../features/auth/screens/create_account_screen.dart';
import '../features/auth/screens/sign_in_screen.dart';
import '../features/auth/screens/unsupported_role_screen.dart';
import '../features/ops/ops_shell.dart';
import '../features/resident/screens/home_screen.dart';
import '../features/responder/responder_shell.dart';
import '../features/home/screens/placeholder_screens.dart';
import 'auth_state_controller.dart';

/// Builds role-aware routes and gates responders by approval state.
GoRouter buildRouter(AuthStateController authState) {
  return GoRouter(
    initialLocation: '/sign-in',
    refreshListenable: authState,
    redirect: (context, state) {
      final isLoading = authState.isLoading;
      if (isLoading) {
        return state.matchedLocation == '/loading' ? null : '/loading';
      }

      final user = authState.firebaseUser;
      final location = state.matchedLocation;

      if (user == null) {
        if (location == '/sign-in' || location == '/create-account') {
          return null;
        }
        return '/sign-in';
      }

      if (authState.isSystemAdmin) {
        return location == '/dispatch-console' ? null : '/dispatch-console';
      }

      final profile = authState.profile;
      if (profile == null) {
        return '/loading';
      }

      final role = profile.role;
      if (role == UserRole.resident) {
        return location == '/home' ? null : '/home';
      }

      // A role this app does not support gets a clear screen, never a blank page or a redirect loop.
      if (role != UserRole.resident && role != UserRole.responder) {
        return location == '/unsupported-role' ? null : '/unsupported-role';
      }

      final responder = authState.responderProfile;
      if (responder == null) {
        return '/pending-verification';
      }

      switch (responder.verificationStatus) {
        case ResponderVerificationStatus.verified:
          return location == '/responder-home' ? null : '/responder-home';
        case ResponderVerificationStatus.pending:
          return location == '/pending-verification' ? null : '/pending-verification';
        case ResponderVerificationStatus.rejected:
          return location == '/registration-rejected' ? null : '/registration-rejected';
        case ResponderVerificationStatus.suspended:
          return location == '/account-suspended' ? null : '/account-suspended';
      }
    },
    routes: [
      GoRoute(
        path: '/loading',
        builder: (context, state) => const LoadingScreen(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/sign-in',
        builder: (context, state) => const SignInScreen(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/create-account',
        builder: (context, state) => const CreateAccountScreen(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/home',
        builder: (context, state) => const ResidentHomeScreen(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/responder-home',
        builder: (context, state) => const ResponderGate(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/pending-verification',
        builder: (context, state) => const PendingVerificationScreen(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/registration-rejected',
        builder: (context, state) => RegistrationRejectedScreen(
          verificationNotes: authState.responderProfile?.verificationNotes,
        ),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/account-suspended',
        builder: (context, state) => const AccountSuspendedScreen(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/dispatch-console',
        builder: (context, state) => const OpsShell(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/admin-dashboard',
        builder: (context, state) => const AdminShell(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/unsupported-role',
        builder: (context, state) => const UnsupportedRoleScreen(),
      ),
    ],
  );
}

##### FILE: mobile\lib\app\permission_service.dart (42 lines)
<<<BEGIN>>>
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:permission_handler/permission_handler.dart';

import '../features/auth/models/auth_models.dart';

/// Requests only the operating-system permissions required by each app role.
class PermissionService {
  bool _hasRequestedResidentPermissions = false;
  bool _hasRequestedResponderPermissions = false;

  /// Requests permissions needed by [role] on supported mobile platforms.
  Future<void> requestForRole(UserRole role) async {
    // Web and desktop platforms do not use the mobile permission prompts.
    if (kIsWeb || !_isMobileTarget) {
      return;
    }

    if (role == UserRole.responder) {
      if (_hasRequestedResponderPermissions) {
        return;
      }
      // Set the guard before awaiting so repeated auth notifications do not prompt twice.
      _hasRequestedResponderPermissions = true;
      await [Permission.notification, Permission.locationWhenInUse].request();
      return;
    }

    if (role == UserRole.resident) {
      if (_hasRequestedResidentPermissions) {
        return;
      }
      _hasRequestedResidentPermissions = true;
      await Permission.notification.request();
    }
  }

  bool get _isMobileTarget {
    return defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.android ||
        defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.iOS;
  }
}

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

/// Represents a timed safety check-in and its Firestore status.
class SafetyCheckIn {
  const SafetyCheckIn({
    required this.id,
    required this.status,
    required this.durationMinutes,
    required this.expiryTime,
  });

  final String id;
  final String status;
  final int durationMinutes;
  final DateTime expiryTime;

  /// Reads the expiration timestamp and duration from a Firestore document.
  factory SafetyCheckIn.fromDocument(
    QueryDocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>> document,
  ) {
    final data = document.data();
    final expiryTime = data['expiryTime'];
    return SafetyCheckIn(
      id: document.id,
      status: data['status'] as String? ?? 'Active',
      durationMinutes: data['durationMinutes'] as int? ?? 0,
      expiryTime: expiryTime is Timestamp ? expiryTime.toDate() : DateTime.now(),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';

/// Represents an owner-managed trusted contact stored in Firestore.
class TrustedContact {
  const TrustedContact({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.phoneNumber,
    required this.relationship,
    required this.isActive,
  });

  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String phoneNumber;
  final String relationship;
  final bool isActive;

  /// Reads contact fields from a Firestore document, applying legacy defaults.
  factory TrustedContact.fromDocument(
    QueryDocumentSnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>> document,
  ) {
    final data = document.data();
    return TrustedContact(
      id: document.id,
      name: data['name'] as String? ?? '',
      phoneNumber: data['phoneNumber'] as String? ?? '',
      relationship: data['relationship'] as String? ?? 'Other',
      isActive: data['isActive'] as bool? ?? false,
    );
  }
}

// The control-room map: incident pins by priority, unit pins by state, and the selected incident's live
// positions (the caller and the units holding the job). Bounds are computed by hand, never newLatLngBounds.
import 'dart:math' as math;
import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'dart:ui' as ui;

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart' show kIsWeb;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:google_maps_flutter/google_maps_flutter.dart';

import '../../core/models/dispatch_models.dart';
import '../resident/map/maps_available.dart';
import 'ops_repository.dart';
import 'ops_tokens.dart';
import 'ops_widgets.dart';

const String kOpsMapStyle = '''
[
  {"elementType":"geometry","stylers":[{"color":"#1a1e22"}]},
  {"elementType":"labels.text.fill","stylers":[{"color":"#8b959e"}]},
  {"elementType":"labels.text.stroke","stylers":[{"color":"#14181b"}]},
  {"featureType":"poi","stylers":[{"visibility":"off"}]},
  {"featureType":"transit","stylers":[{"visibility":"off"}]},
  {"featureType":"road","elementType":"geometry","stylers":[{"color":"#2b3238"}]},
  {"featureType":"road.arterial","elementType":"geometry","stylers":[{"color":"#333b42"}]},
  {"featureType":"road.highway","elementType":"geometry","stylers":[{"color":"#3d454d"}]},
  {"featureType":"road","elementType":"labels.icon","stylers":[{"visibility":"off"}]},
  {"featureType":"water","elementType":"geometry","stylers":[{"color":"#14262e"}]},
  {"featureType":"administrative.land_parcel","stylers":[{"visibility":"off"}]}
]
''';

IconData _categoryIcon(String category) => switch (category) {
  'Medical Emergency' => Icons.local_hospital_outlined,
  'Road Accident' => Icons.directions_car_outlined,
  'Fire & Rescue' => Icons.local_fire_department_outlined,
  'Security Threat' || 'Abduction' => Icons.shield_outlined,
  _ => Icons.warning_amber_rounded,
};

const Color _onJob = Color(0xFFFFB020);

class OpsMap extends StatefulWidget {
  const OpsMap({super.key, required this.incidents, required this.responders, required this.selectedId, required this.onSelect, this.tracks = const {}, this.residentUid});
  final List<IncidentRecord> incidents;
  final List<ResponderRecord> responders;
  final String? selectedId;
  final void Function(String id) onSelect;
  final Map<String, TrackPoint> tracks;
  final String? residentUid;

  @override
  State<OpsMap> createState() => _OpsMapState();
}

class _OpsMapState extends State<OpsMap> {
  final Map<String, BitmapDescriptor> _pins = {};
  GoogleMapController? _map;
  String _fitKey = '';

  IncidentRecord? get _selected {
    for (final i in widget.incidents) {
      if (i.id == widget.selectedId) return i;
    }
    return null;
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    _ensurePins();
  }

  /// Pins are drawn once per priority+category and unit state, then reused (web-safe: no default marker glyphs).
  Future<void> _ensurePins() async {
    final wanted = <String, (Color, IconData)>{};
    for (final i in widget.incidents) {
      wanted['p_${i.priority}_${i.category}'] = (OT.priority(i.priority), _categoryIcon(i.category));
    }
    wanted['u_Available'] = (OT.accent, Icons.navigation_outlined);
    wanted['u_On a job'] = (_onJob, Icons.navigation_outlined);
    wanted['u_Stale GPS'] = (const Color(0xFF8A939B), Icons.navigation_outlined);
    wanted['u_Offline'] = (const Color(0xFF5A636B), Icons.navigation_outlined);
    wanted['live'] = (OT.accent, Icons.navigation);
    wanted['resident'] = (const Color(0xFFB388FF), Icons.person_pin_circle);
    var added = false;
    for (final e in wanted.entries) {
      if (_pins.containsKey(e.key)) continue;
      _pins[e.key] = await _pin(e.key, e.value.$1, e.value.$2);
      added = true;
    }
    if (added && mounted) setState(() {});
  }

  Future<BitmapDescriptor> _pin(String key, Color color, IconData icon) async {
    final hit = _pins[key];
    if (hit != null) return hit;
    const scale = 2.0;
    const logical = 38.0;
    final size = logical * scale;
    final rec = ui.PictureRecorder();
    final c = Canvas(rec);
    final center = Offset(size / 2, size / 2);
    c.drawCircle(center + const Offset(0, 2), size / 2 - 3, Paint()..color = const Color(0x55000000));
    c.drawCircle(center, size / 2 - 3, Paint()..color = const Color(0xFFEDEFF1));
    c.drawCircle(center, size / 2 - 7, Paint()..color = color);
    final tp = TextPainter(
      text: TextSpan(text: String.fromCharCode(icon.codePoint), style: TextStyle(fontSize: size * 0.42, fontFamily: icon.fontFamily, package: icon.fontPackage, color: Colors.white)),
      textDirection: TextDirection.ltr,
    )..layout();
    tp.paint(c, center - Offset(tp.width / 2, tp.height / 2));
    final img = await rec.endRecording().toImage(size.toInt(), size.toInt());
    final data = await img.toByteData(format: ui.ImageByteFormat.png);
    if (data == null) return BitmapDescriptor.defaultMarker;
    final d = BitmapDescriptor.bytes(Uint8List.view(data.buffer), width: logical, height: logical);
    _pins[key] = d;
    return d;
  }

  Set<Marker> _markers() {
    final out = <Marker>{};
    for (final i in widget.incidents) {
      if (i.latitude == null || i.longitude == null) continue;
      out.add(Marker(
        markerId: MarkerId('i_${i.id}'),
        position: LatLng(i.latitude!, i.longitude!),
        icon: _pins['p_${i.priority}_${i.category}'] ?? BitmapDescriptor.defaultMarkerWithHue(_hue(i.priority)),
        onTap: () => widget.onSelect(i.id),
        infoWindow: InfoWindow(title: '${i.priority} · ${i.category}', snippet: 'Tap to open'),
        zIndexInt: widget.selectedId == i.id ? 6 : 4,
      ));
    }
    final now = DateTime.now();
    for (final r in widget.responders) {
      if (r.latitude == null || r.longitude == null) continue;
      final group = unitGroup(r.availabilityStatus, r.lastSeenAt, now: now);
      out.add(Marker(
        markerId: MarkerId('u_${r.id}'),
        position: LatLng(r.latitude!, r.longitude!),
        icon: _pins['u_$group'] ?? BitmapDescriptor.defaultMarkerWithHue(_unitHue(group)),
        infoWindow: InfoWindow(title: r.organizationName, snippet: '$group · ${r.organizationType}${r.lastSeenAt == null ? '' : ' · seen ${_ago(r.lastSeenAt!)}'}'),
        zIndexInt: 2,
      ));
    }
    final sel = _selected;
    if (sel != null) {
      for (final t in widget.tracks.values) {
        final mine = t.ownerId == widget.residentUid;
        if (mine) continue;
        out.add(Marker(
          markerId: MarkerId('t_${t.ownerId}'),
          position: LatLng(t.latitude, t.longitude),
          icon: _pins['live'] ?? BitmapDescriptor.defaultMarkerWithHue(BitmapDescriptor.hueAzure),
          infoWindow: const InfoWindow(title: 'Live unit position'),
          zIndexInt: 7,
        ));
      }
      final home = widget.tracks[widget.residentUid];
      if (home != null) {
        out.add(Marker(
          markerId: const MarkerId('resident_live'),
          position: LatLng(home.latitude, home.longitude),
          icon: _pins['resident'] ?? BitmapDescriptor.defaultMarkerWithHue(BitmapDescriptor.hueViolet),
          infoWindow: const InfoWindow(title: 'Caller (live position)'),
          zIndexInt: 8,
        ));
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  static double _hue(String priority) => switch (priority) { 'High' => BitmapDescriptor.hueRed, 'Medium' => BitmapDescriptor.hueOrange, _ => BitmapDescriptor.hueBlue };
  static double _unitHue(String group) => switch (group) { 'Available' => BitmapDescriptor.hueGreen, 'On a job' => BitmapDescriptor.hueYellow, _ => BitmapDescriptor.hueRose };

  List<LatLng> _points() {
    final pts = <LatLng>[];
    for (final i in widget.incidents) {
      if (i.latitude != null && i.longitude != null) pts.add(LatLng(i.latitude!, i.longitude!));
    }
    final sel = _selected;
    if (sel != null) {
      for (final t in widget.tracks.values) {
        pts.add(LatLng(t.latitude, t.longitude));
      }
    }
    return pts;
  }

  /// Same manual zoom as the help map: frame the points, never the whole city, clamp 10..17.
  void _fit() {
    if (_map == null || !mounted) return;
    final pts = _points();
    if (pts.isEmpty) return;
    final key = '${widget.selectedId}|${pts.length}|${pts.first.latitude.toStringAsFixed(4)}';
    if (key == _fitKey) return;
    _fitKey = key;
    if (pts.length == 1) {
      _move(pts.first, 14.5);
      return;
    }
    var minLa = pts.first.latitude, maxLa = minLa, minLo = pts.first.longitude, maxLo = minLo;
    for (final p in pts) {
      minLa = math.min(minLa, p.latitude);
      maxLa = math.max(maxLa, p.latitude);
      minLo = math.min(minLo, p.longitude);
      maxLo = math.max(maxLo, p.longitude);
    }
    final box = context.size ?? const Size(800, 600);
    final usableH = (box.height - 40).clamp(200.0, 4000.0);
    final usableW = (box.width - 40).clamp(200.0, 4000.0);
    double y(double lat) {
      final sn = math.sin(lat * math.pi / 180);
      return math.log((1 + sn) / (1 - sn)) / 2;
    }
    final dLo = math.max((maxLo - minLo).abs(), 0.0006);
    final dY = math.max((y(maxLa) - y(minLa)).abs(), 0.00001);
    final zLo = math.log(usableW * 360 / (256 * dLo)) / math.ln2;
    final zLa = math.log(usableH * 2 * math.pi / (256 * dY)) / math.ln2;
    final zoom = (math.min(zLo, zLa) - 0.3).clamp(10.0, 17.0);
    _move(LatLng((minLa + maxLa) / 2, (minLo + maxLo) / 2), zoom);
  }

  Future<void> _move(LatLng c, double zoom) async {
    for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
      try {
        await _map?.animateCamera(CameraUpdate.newLatLngZoom(c, zoom));
        return;
      } catch (_) {
        await Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 250));
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(covariant OpsMap old) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(old);
    if (old.incidents.length != widget.incidents.length) _ensurePins();
    if (old.selectedId != widget.selectedId && widget.selectedId != null) {
      final sel = _selected;
      if (sel != null && sel.latitude != null && sel.longitude != null) _move(LatLng(sel.latitude!, sel.longitude!), 15.5);
    } else {
      _fit();
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    if (kIsWeb && !hasGoogleMapsRuntime()) {
      return const EmptyState(icon: Icons.map_outlined, title: 'Map unavailable', message: 'The Google Maps JavaScript API key is not configured for this browser. Queue, units and incident details still work.');
    }
    final initial = _points();
    final sel = _selected;
    final start = sel?.latitude != null ? LatLng(sel!.latitude!, sel.longitude!) : (initial.isNotEmpty ? initial.first : const LatLng(-1.2921, 36.8219));
    return LayoutBuilder(builder: (context, c) {
      return ClipRRect(
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(OT.radius),
        child: Stack(children: [
          GoogleMap(
            initialCameraPosition: CameraPosition(target: start, zoom: 13),
            style: kOpsMapStyle,
            markers: _markers(),
            minMaxZoomPreference: const MinMaxZoomPreference(10, 19),
            zoomControlsEnabled: true,
            mapToolbarEnabled: false,
            compassEnabled: false,
            myLocationButtonEnabled: false,
            onMapCreated: (m) {
              _map = m;
              _fit();
              Future<void>.delayed(const Duration(milliseconds: 600), () {
                if (mounted) {
                  _fitKey = '';
                  _fit();
                }
              });
            },
          ),
          Positioned(left: 10, bottom: 10, child: _legend()),
        ]),
      );
    });
  }

  Widget _legend() => Container(
    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
    decoration: BoxDecoration(color: OT.panel.withValues(alpha: 0.94), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10), border: Border.all(color: OT.line)),
    child: const Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
      _LegendDot(OT.alert, 'High'),
      SizedBox(width: 10),
      _LegendDot(Color(0xFFFFB020), 'Medium'),
      SizedBox(width: 10),
      _LegendDot(Color(0xFF5AB0FF), 'Low'),
      SizedBox(width: 10),
      _LegendDot(OT.accent, 'Unit ready'),
    ]),
  );
}

class _LegendDot extends StatelessWidget {
  const _LegendDot(this.color, this.label);
  final Color color;
  final String label;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Row(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
    Container(width: 8, height: 8, decoration: BoxDecoration(color: color, shape: BoxShape.circle)),
    const SizedBox(width: 4),
    Text(label, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 10.5, color: OT.muted)),
  ]);
}

String _ago(DateTime t) {
  final d = DateTime.now().difference(t);
  if (d.inSeconds < 60) return '${d.inSeconds}s ago';
  if (d.inMinutes < 60) return '${d.inMinutes} min ago';
  return '${d.inHours} h ago';
}

// The bell tray: SOS, attention flags, extra-unit requests, new High incidents and imprecise locations.
// A new High incident or SOS rings the system sound ONCE (never on load, never while muted).
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../core/models/dispatch_models.dart';
import 'ops_repository.dart';
import 'ops_tokens.dart';
import 'ops_widgets.dart';

enum NoticeKind { sos, attention, extraResources, high, lowAccuracy }

class OpsNotice {
  const OpsNotice({required this.id, required this.kind, required this.title, required this.body, required this.at, this.incidentId, this.sos});

  final String id;
  final NoticeKind kind;
  final String title;
  final String body;
  final DateTime? at;
  final String? incidentId;
  final SosAlert? sos;

  bool get isUrgent => kind == NoticeKind.sos || kind == NoticeKind.high;
}

/// Newest first. An incident can raise more than one notice (e.g. High priority and low accuracy).
List<OpsNotice> buildNotices({required List<IncidentRecord> incidents, required List<SosAlert> sos, DateTime? now}) {
  final out = <OpsNotice>[];
  for (final s in sos) {
    if (s.status != 'open') continue;
    out.add(OpsNotice(
      id: 'sos_${s.id}',
      kind: NoticeKind.sos,
      title: 'SOS from ${s.orgName}',
      body: s.incidentId == null ? 'A responder asked for immediate help.' : 'A responder on an incident asked for immediate help.',
      at: s.at,
      incidentId: s.incidentId,
      sos: s,
    ));
  }
  for (final i in incidents) {
    if (!i.isOpen) continue;
    final where = i.landmark.isEmpty ? i.category : '${i.category} · ${i.landmark}';
    if (i.needsAttention) {
      final kind = i.attentionReason == 'extra_resources'
          ? NoticeKind.extraResources
          : i.attentionReason == 'low_accuracy'
          ? NoticeKind.lowAccuracy
          : NoticeKind.attention;
      final body = switch (i.attentionReason) {
        'no_accept_in_time' => 'No unit has accepted within the target time.',
        'extra_resources' => 'Someone asked for more units.',
        'responder_sos' => 'A responder raised an SOS.',
        'low_accuracy' => 'The caller location is imprecise.',
        _ => 'This incident was flagged for a human.',
      };
      out.add(OpsNotice(id: '${kind.name}_${i.id}', kind: kind, title: where, body: body, at: i.createdAt, incidentId: i.id));
    }
    if (i.priority == 'High') {
      out.add(OpsNotice(id: 'high_${i.id}', kind: NoticeKind.high, title: i.isUnassessed ? 'New alert: $where' : 'High priority: $where', body: i.isUnassessed ? 'Details not given yet. Treat as urgent until they arrive.' : 'A new High priority incident is open.', at: i.createdAt, incidentId: i.id));
    }
  }
  out.sort((a, b) => (b.at ?? DateTime(2000)).compareTo(a.at ?? DateTime(2000)));
  return out;
}

IconData noticeIcon(NoticeKind k) => switch (k) {
  NoticeKind.sos => Icons.sos_outlined,
  NoticeKind.attention => Icons.priority_high,
  NoticeKind.extraResources => Icons.group_add_outlined,
  NoticeKind.high => Icons.campaign_outlined,
  NoticeKind.lowAccuracy => Icons.my_location_outlined,
};

class NoticeTray extends StatelessWidget {
  const NoticeTray({super.key, required this.notices, required this.muted, required this.onToggleMute, required this.onOpenIncident, required this.onResolveSos, required this.onClose});

  final List<OpsNotice> notices;
  final bool muted;
  final VoidCallback onToggleMute;
  final void Function(String incidentId) onOpenIncident;
  final void Function(SosAlert sos) onResolveSos;
  final VoidCallback onClose;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Container(
    width: 380,
    constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxHeight: 520),
    decoration: BoxDecoration(color: OT.panel, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(OT.radius), border: Border.all(color: OT.line)),
    child: Column(mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min, children: [
      Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 12, 8, 8),
        child: Row(children: [
          const Expanded(child: Text('Notifications', style: TextStyle(fontSize: 15, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800, color: OT.text))),
          TextButton.icon(
            onPressed: onToggleMute,
            icon: Icon(muted ? Icons.volume_off_outlined : Icons.volume_up_outlined, size: 18),
            label: Text(muted ? 'Muted' : 'Sound on'),
          ),
          IconButton(tooltip: 'Close', onPressed: onClose, icon: const Icon(Icons.close, size: 18)),
        ]),
      ),
      const Divider(height: 1, color: OT.line),
      Flexible(
        child: notices.isEmpty
            ? const EmptyState(icon: Icons.notifications_none, title: 'Nothing needs you right now', message: 'SOS alerts, attention flags and new High priority incidents appear here.')
            : ListView.separated(
                shrinkWrap: true,
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
                itemCount: notices.length,
                separatorBuilder: (_, __) => const SizedBox(height: 6),
                itemBuilder: (_, k) {
                  final n = notices[k];
                  final urgent = n.kind == NoticeKind.sos;
                  return Material(
                    color: urgent ? const Color(0xFF2C1A1A) : OT.raised,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    child: InkWell(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                      onTap: () {
                        if (n.sos != null) {
                          onResolveSos(n.sos!);
                        } else if (n.incidentId != null) {
                          onOpenIncident(n.incidentId!);
                        }
                      },
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
                        child: Row(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                          Container(
                            width: 26,
                            height: 26,
                            alignment: Alignment.center,
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: urgent ? OT.alert : OT.panel, shape: BoxShape.circle, border: Border.all(color: urgent ? OT.alert : OT.line)),
                            child: Icon(noticeIcon(n.kind), size: 14, color: urgent ? Colors.white : OT.soft),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: 10),
                          Expanded(
                            child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                              Text(n.title, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 13, fontWeight: FontWeight.w700, color: OT.text)),
                              const SizedBox(height: 2),
                              Text(n.body, style: const TextStyle(fontSize: 12, color: OT.muted)),
                            ]),
                          ),
                          if (n.sos != null)
                            TextButton(onPressed: () => onResolveSos(n.sos!), child: const Text('Resolve')),
                        ]),
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
      ),
    ]),
  );
}

// A card for the "Help is being sent" screen that opens the map. Shows a one-line live summary and never errors.
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import 'help_map_screen.dart';
import 'help_models.dart';
import 'help_repository.dart';

class HelpMapEntry extends StatelessWidget {
  const HelpMapEntry({super.key, required this.incidentId});
  final String incidentId;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return StreamBuilder<HelpState>(
      stream: HelpRepository().watchOptions(incidentId),
      builder: (context, snap) {
        final s = snap.data;
        final n = s?.options.length ?? 0;
        final live = s?.options.where((o) => o.isLive).length ?? 0;
        final best = s?.options.map((o) => o.etaMin).whereType<double>().fold<double?>(null, (a, b) => a == null || b < a ? b : a);
        final line = s == null || !s.loaded
            ? 'Opening the map…'
            : n == 0
                ? 'See emergency numbers and nearby help'
                : '$n option${n == 1 ? '' : 's'}${live > 0 ? ' · $live live' : ''}${best != null ? ' · nearest about ${best.round().clamp(1, 999)} min' : ''}';
        return InkWell(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          onTap: () => Navigator.of(context).push(MaterialPageRoute<void>(builder: (_) => HelpMapScreen(incidentId: incidentId))),
          child: Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: const Color(0xFF0F766E), borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20)),
            child: Row(children: [
              const Icon(Icons.map_outlined, color: Colors.white, size: 30),
              const SizedBox(width: 14),
              Expanded(child: Column(crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start, children: [
                const Text('See help on the map', style: TextStyle(color: Colors.white, fontSize: 17, fontWeight: FontWeight.w800)),
                const SizedBox(height: 2),
                Text(line, style: const TextStyle(color: Color(0xFFD7F0EC), fontSize: 13)),
              ])),
              const Icon(Icons.chevron_right, color: Colors.white),
            ]),
          ),
        );
      },
    );
  }
}

// Emergency help map (v3). Order of events: 1) find the resident (LocationGate) 2) show ONLY places that matter for THIS emergency
// 3) the engine's top-3 options on top, then nearby places by type, 4) live tracking of the unit that accepted.
// Data: options/tracks written by Cloud Functions; directory places read from Firestore. This screen decides nothing about who is sent.
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:math' as math;

import 'package:cloud_functions/cloud_functions.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart' show kIsWeb;
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:google_maps_flutter/google_maps_flutter.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';

import '../../../core/ui/glass.dart';
import '../../../core/ui/tokens.dart';
