import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:intl_phone_number_input/intl_phone_number_input.dart';

import '../../../core/models/trusted_contact.dart';
import '../../../core/services/phone_number_service.dart';
import '../../../core/theme/app_theme.dart';

/// Lists and manages the signed-in resident's trusted contacts.
class TrustedContactsScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const TrustedContactsScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<TrustedContactsScreen> createState() => _TrustedContactsScreenState();
}

class _TrustedContactsScreenState extends State<TrustedContactsScreen> {
  int _queryRevision = 0;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final userId = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser!.uid;
    // Soft-deleted contacts stay in Firestore but are hidden from this list.
    final contacts = FirebaseFirestore.instance
        .collection('trustedContacts')
        .where('userId', isEqualTo: userId)
        .where('isActive', isEqualTo: true)
        .snapshots();

    return StreamBuilder<QuerySnapshot<Map<String, dynamic>>>(
      key: ValueKey(_queryRevision),
      stream: contacts,
      builder: (context, snapshot) {
        if (snapshot.hasError) {
          debugPrint('Trusted contacts query failed: ${snapshot.error}');
          return Center(
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              child: Column(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  const Icon(
                    Icons.cloud_off_outlined,
                    size: 48,
                    color: AppColors.textTertiary,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
                  Text(
                    'Unable to load trusted contacts.',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.sm),
                  Text(
                    'Check your connection and try again.',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.bodyMedium,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: AppSpacing.md),
                  TextButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () => setState(() => _queryRevision++),
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.refresh),
                    label: const Text('Retry'),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          );
        }
        if (!snapshot.hasData) {
          return const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator());
        }

        final contacts = snapshot.data!.docs
            .map(TrustedContact.fromDocument)
            .toList();
        return CustomScrollView(
          slivers: [
            SliverPadding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 24, 20, 12),
              sliver: SliverToBoxAdapter(
                child: Row(
                  children: [
                    Expanded(
                      child: Text(
                        'Trusted Contacts',
                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
                      ),
                    ),
                    FilledButton.icon(
                      onPressed: () => _editContact(context, userId: userId),
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
                      label: const Text('Add contact'),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            if (contacts.isEmpty)
              const SliverFillRemaining(
                hasScrollBody: false,
                child: Center(child: Text('Add someone you trust to your list.')),
              )
            else
              SliverPadding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 4, 12, 24),
                sliver: SliverList.separated(
                  itemCount: contacts.length,
                  separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const Divider(height: 1),
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                    final contact = contacts[index];
                    return Dismissible(
                      key: ValueKey(contact.id),
                      direction: DismissDirection.endToStart,
                      background: Container(
                        alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 20),
                        color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.errorContainer,
                        child: Icon(
                          Icons.delete_outline,
                          color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.onErrorContainer,
                        ),
                      ),
                      confirmDismiss: (_) => showDialog<bool>(
                        context: context,
                        builder: (context) => AlertDialog(
                          title: const Text('Remove trusted contact?'),
                          content: Text('${contact.name} will be removed from your active list.'),
                          actions: [
                            TextButton(
                              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, false),
                              child: const Text('Cancel'),
                            ),
                            FilledButton(
                              onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context, true),
                              child: const Text('Remove'),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      // Retain contact history while removing the entry from active lists.
                      onDismissed: (_) => FirebaseFirestore.instance
                          .collection('trustedContacts')
                          .doc(contact.id)
                          .update({
                            'isActive': false,
                            'updatedAt': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
                          }),
                      child: ListTile(
                        minVerticalPadding: 12,
                        leading: CircleAvatar(
                          backgroundColor: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.secondaryContainer,
                          child: Text(_initials(contact.name)),
                        ),
                        title: Text(contact.name),
                        subtitle: Text('${contact.relationship}  ·  ${contact.phoneNumber}'),
                        trailing: IconButton(
                          tooltip: 'Edit contact',
                          icon: const Icon(Icons.edit_outlined),
                          onPressed: () => _editContact(
                            context,
                            userId: userId,
                            contact: contact,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                ),
              ),
          ],
        );
      },
    );
  }

  Future<void> _editContact(
    BuildContext context, {
    required String userId,
    TrustedContact? contact,
  }) async {
    await showModalBottomSheet<void>(
      context: context,
      isScrollControlled: true,
      useSafeArea: true,
      builder: (context) => _TrustedContactForm(
        contact: contact,
        onSave: (name, phoneNumber, relationship) async {
          final reference = contact == null
              ? FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('trustedContacts').doc()
              : FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('trustedContacts').doc(contact.id);
          final now = FieldValue.serverTimestamp();
          // New records start active; edits leave ownership and creation time unchanged.
          if (contact == null) {
            await reference.set({
              'userId': userId,
              'name': name,
              'phoneNumber': phoneNumber,
              'relationship': relationship,
              'isActive': true,
              'createdAt': now,
              'updatedAt': now,
            });
          } else {
            await reference.update({
              'name': name,
              'phoneNumber': phoneNumber,
              'relationship': relationship,
              'updatedAt': now,
            });
          }
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  String _initials(String name) {
    final parts = name.trim().split(RegExp(r'\s+')).where((part) => part.isNotEmpty);
    return parts.take(2).map((part) => part[0].toUpperCase()).join();
  }
}

class _TrustedContactForm extends StatefulWidget {
  const _TrustedContactForm({required this.onSave, this.contact});

  final TrustedContact? contact;
  final Future<void> Function(String name, String phoneNumber, String relationship)
  onSave;

  @override
  State<_TrustedContactForm> createState() => _TrustedContactFormState();
}

class _TrustedContactFormState extends State<_TrustedContactForm> {
  static const _relationships = [
    'Sister',
    'Brother',
    'Friend',
    'Parent',
    'Spouse',
    'Other',
  ];

  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _nameController = TextEditingController();
  final _phoneController = TextEditingController();
  final _phoneNumberService = PhoneNumberService();
  String _countryCode = 'KE';
  String _relationship = 'Other';
  bool _saving = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final contact = widget.contact;
    if (contact != null) {
      _nameController.text = contact.name;
      _phoneController.text = contact.phoneNumber;
      _relationship = contact.relationship;
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _phoneController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _save() async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) {
      return;
    }
    setState(() => _saving = true);
    try {
      // Persist one canonical format regardless of how the user entered the number.
      final phoneNumber = _phoneNumberService.formatToE164(
        _phoneController.text,
        _countryCode,
      );
      await widget.onSave(
        _nameController.text.trim(),
        phoneNumber,
        _relationship,
      );
      if (mounted) {
        Navigator.pop(context);
      }
    } catch (_) {
      if (mounted) {
        ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
          const SnackBar(content: Text('Unable to save contact. Please try again.')),
        );
        setState(() => _saving = false);
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    // Keep the focused field visible when the keyboard opens the bottom sheet.
    final bottomInset = MediaQuery.viewInsetsOf(context).bottom;
    return Padding(
      padding: EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 20, 20, 20 + bottomInset),
      child: Form(
        key: _formKey,
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Text(
              widget.contact == null ? 'Add trusted contact' : 'Edit trusted contact',
              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            TextFormField(
              controller: _nameController,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Name',
                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              ),
              validator: (value) => value == null || value.trim().isEmpty
                  ? 'Name is required'
                  : null,
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            InternationalPhoneNumberInput(
              textFieldController: _phoneController,
              initialValue: PhoneNumber(
                phoneNumber: widget.contact?.phoneNumber,
                isoCode: 'KE',
              ),
              maxLength: 25,
              selectorConfig: const SelectorConfig(
                selectorType: PhoneInputSelectorType.BOTTOM_SHEET,
                useEmoji: true,
              ),
              autoValidateMode: AutovalidateMode.onUserInteraction,
              inputDecoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Phone Number',
                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              ),
              onInputChanged: (number) {
                _countryCode = number.isoCode ?? 'KE';
              },
              validator: (value) => _phoneNumberService.validate(value, _countryCode),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 12),
            DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
              initialValue: _relationship,
              decoration: const InputDecoration(
                labelText: 'Relationship',
                border: OutlineInputBorder(),
              ),
              items: _relationships
                  .map((value) => DropdownMenuItem(value: value, child: Text(value)))
                  .toList(),
              onChanged: (value) {
                if (value != null) {
                  setState(() => _relationship = value);
                }
              },
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 16),
            FilledButton(
              onPressed: _saving ? null : _save,
              child: Text(_saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save contact'),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

// Shares a responder's live position with the resident ONLY while they hold a job (accepted / en route / arrived).
// On Android this runs as a foreground service (a visible notification) so the system does not stop it. Stops by itself when the job ends.
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:cloud_firestore/cloud_firestore.dart';
import 'package:firebase_auth/firebase_auth.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:geolocator/geolocator.dart';

import '../../core/models/dispatch_models.dart';

class TrackPublisher {
  TrackPublisher._();
  static final TrackPublisher instance = TrackPublisher._();

  StreamSubscription<Position>? _sub;
  Timer? _keepAlive;
  Set<String> _incidents = {};
  Position? _last;

  /// Shown to the responder if tracking cannot run (null = fine).
  final ValueNotifier<String?> problem = ValueNotifier<String?>(null);

  bool get running => _sub != null;

  /// Call with the responder's full offers list every time it changes.
  void sync(Iterable<Offer> offers) {
    final want = <String>{for (final o in offers) if (o.status == 'accepted' || o.status == 'enroute' || o.status == 'arrived') o.incidentId};
    if (setEquals(want, _incidents)) return;
    _incidents = want;
    if (want.isEmpty) {
      stop();
    } else if (_sub == null) {
      _start();
    } else {
      _write(_last); // a second job started: publish immediately for it too
    }
  }

  Future<void> _start() async {
    try {
      var p = await Geolocator.checkPermission();
      if (p == LocationPermission.denied) p = await Geolocator.requestPermission();
      if (p == LocationPermission.denied || p == LocationPermission.deniedForever || !await Geolocator.isLocationServiceEnabled()) {
        problem.value = 'Turn on location so the person you are helping can see you coming.';
        return;
      }
      final LocationSettings settings = defaultTargetPlatform == TargetPlatform.android
          ? AndroidSettings(
              accuracy: LocationAccuracy.high,
              distanceFilter: 20,
              intervalDuration: const Duration(seconds: 5),
              foregroundNotificationConfig: const ForegroundNotificationConfig(
                notificationTitle: 'Emergency job in progress',
                notificationText: 'Sharing your live location with the person you are helping.',
                enableWakeLock: true,
              ),
            )
          : const LocationSettings(accuracy: LocationAccuracy.high, distanceFilter: 20);
      _sub = Geolocator.getPositionStream(locationSettings: settings).listen((pos) {
        _last = pos;
        _write(pos);
      }, onError: (Object _) => problem.value = 'Lost your location signal.');
      _keepAlive = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 15), (_) => _write(_last)); // standing still still counts as "live"
      problem.value = null;
    } catch (_) {
      problem.value = 'Could not start live location.';
    }
  }

  Future<void> _write(Position? pos) async {
    final uid = FirebaseAuth.instance.currentUser?.uid;
    if (pos == null || uid == null) return;
    for (final iid in _incidents) {
      try {
        await FirebaseFirestore.instance.collection('incidents').doc(iid).collection('tracks').doc(uid).set({
          'lat': pos.latitude,
          'lng': pos.longitude,
          'heading': pos.heading,
          'speed': pos.speed,
          'accuracy': pos.accuracy,
          'at': FieldValue.serverTimestamp(),
        });
      } catch (_) {/* offline or just finished: the next fix tries again */}
    }
  }

  void stop() {
    _sub?.cancel();
    _sub = null;
    _keepAlive?.cancel();
    _keepAlive = null;
    _incidents = {};
    _last = null;
  }
}

=== 5. firestore.rules (whole) and indexes ===

##### FILE: firestore.rules (676 lines)
<<<BEGIN>>>
rules_version = '2';
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Helpers
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    function isSignedIn() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    function isOwner(userId) {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.uid == userId;
    }

    // Admin access comes from a server-assigned claim, never a client-editable profile field.
    function isSystemAdmin() {
      return isSignedIn() && request.auth.token.role == "SystemAdmin";
    }

    function validPhoneNumber(phoneNumber) {
      return phoneNumber is string && phoneNumber.matches('^\\+[1-9]\\d{1,14}$');
    }

    function validPreferredLanguage(language) {
      return language in ["en", "sw"];
    }

    function validPublicRole(role) {
      return role in ["Resident", "Responder"];
    }

    function validAccountStatus(status) {
      return status in ["Active", "PendingVerification", "Suspended"];
    }

    function validResponderType(orgType) {
      return orgType in [
        "Ambulance",
        "Fire",
        "Police",
        "Hospital",
        "Community",
        "Maritime",
        "Other"
      ];
    }

    function validResponderVerificationStatus(status) {
      return status in ["Pending", "Verified", "Rejected", "Suspended"];
    }

    function validAvailabilityStatus(status) {
      return status in ["Available", "Busy", "Offline"];
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Trusted contacts and safety check-ins (unchanged)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    function validTrustedContactData() {
      return request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly([
          "userId",
          "name",
          "phoneNumber",
          "relationship",
          "isActive",
          "createdAt",
          "updatedAt"
        ])
        && request.resource.data.keys().hasAll([
          "userId",
          "name",
          "phoneNumber",
          "relationship",
          "isActive",
          "createdAt",
          "updatedAt"
        ])
        && request.resource.data.name is string
        && request.resource.data.name.size() > 0
        && request.resource.data.name.size() <= 100
        && validPhoneNumber(request.resource.data.phoneNumber)
        && request.resource.data.relationship in ["Sister", "Brother", "Friend", "Parent", "Spouse", "Other"]
        && request.resource.data.isActive is bool
        && request.resource.data.createdAt is timestamp
        && request.resource.data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    function validSafetyCheckinCreate() {
      return request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly([
          "userId",
          "startTime",
          "expiryTime",
          "status",
          "durationMinutes",
          "createdAt",
          "updatedAt"
        ])
        && request.resource.data.keys().hasAll([
          "userId",
          "startTime",
          "expiryTime",
          "status",
          "durationMinutes",
          "createdAt",
          "updatedAt"
        ])
        && request.resource.data.startTime is timestamp
        && request.resource.data.expiryTime is timestamp
        && request.resource.data.status == "Active"
        && request.resource.data.durationMinutes is int
        && request.resource.data.durationMinutes in [15, 30, 45, 60]
        && request.resource.data.createdAt is timestamp
        && request.resource.data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Incidents (emergency alerts)
    //
    // Life cycle written by the resident's phone:
    //   1. CREATE  at the end of the 5-second countdown: always the fail-safe (High, pending review).
    //   2. UPDATE  "quick": the 3 quick answers (and the category if it was changed). Priority stays High.
    //   3. UPDATE  "complete": all details; the priority may now be refined by the decision table.
    //   4. UPDATE  "cancel": the resident reports it was a mistake or they are safe.
    // Everything else (dispatch plan, reference, responders) is written by Cloud Functions, which bypass these rules.
    // Responders never read this collection. They read their own briefing in /offers instead.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    function validIncidentCategory(category) {
      return category in [
        "Medical Emergency",
        "Road Accident",
        "Fire & Rescue",
        "Security Threat",
        "Abduction",
        "Other / General"
      ];
    }

    function validIncidentCreate() {
      let d = request.resource.data;
      return d.keys().hasOnly([
          "userId", "category", "status", "location", "createdAt", "updatedAt",
          "priority", "prioritySource", "needsHumanReview", "triageStatus",
          "landmark", "locationAccuracyM", "locationSource"
        ])
        && d.keys().hasAll([
          "userId", "category", "status", "location", "createdAt", "updatedAt",
          "priority", "prioritySource", "needsHumanReview", "triageStatus"
        ])
        && validIncidentCategory(d.category)
        && d.status == "Active"
        && (d.location == null || d.location is latlng)
        && d.createdAt is timestamp
        && d.updatedAt is timestamp
        && d.priority == "High"
        && d.prioritySource == "safe_fallback"
        && d.needsHumanReview == true
        && d.triageStatus == "pending"
        && validIncidentContext(d);
    }

    // Where the alert is, in words the caller recognises. Optional; never required to send an alert.
    function validIncidentContext(d) {
      return (d.get("landmark", "") is string && d.get("landmark", "").size() <= 120)
        && (d.get("locationAccuracyM", 0) is number)
        && (d.get("locationSource", "gps") in ["gps", "manual_pin"]);
    }

    function incidentChange() {
      return request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys();
    }

    // Step 2: quick answers only. May repeat (the phone saves after every answer). The priority cannot change here (it is not an allowed key).
    function validIncidentQuickUpdate() {
      let d = request.resource.data;
      return incidentChange().hasOnly(["category", "subtype", "triage", "triageStatus", "updatedAt"])
        && resource.data.get("triageStatus", "") in ["pending", "quick"]
        && resource.data.status in ["Active", "SupportOnly"]
        && d.triageStatus == "quick"
        && validIncidentCategory(d.category)
        && (d.get("subtype", "") == null || d.get("subtype", "") is string)
        && d.triage is map
        && d.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // Step 3: full details. Allowed from "pending" (the resident skipped the quick step) or from "quick".
    function validIncidentCompleteUpdate() {
      let d = request.resource.data;
      return incidentChange().hasOnly([
          "category", "subtype", "priority", "prioritySource", "needsHumanReview",
          "triageStatus", "triage", "description", "flags", "status", "updatedAt"
        ])
        && resource.data.get("triageStatus", "") in ["pending", "quick"]
        && resource.data.status in ["Active", "SupportOnly"]
        && d.triageStatus == "complete"
        && validIncidentCategory(d.category)
        && d.priority in ["Low", "Medium", "High"]
        && d.prioritySource is string
        && d.prioritySource.size() <= 40
        && d.needsHumanReview is bool
        && d.triage is map
        && d.status in ["Active", "SupportOnly"]
        && (d.get("subtype", "") == null || d.get("subtype", "") is string)
        && (d.get("description", "") is string && d.get("description", "").size() <= 280)
        && (d.get("flags", []) is list && d.get("flags", []).size() <= 10)
        && d.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // Step 2b: a GPS fix that arrived AFTER the alert was saved. Only the location (and updatedAt) may change.
    function validIncidentLocationUpdate() {
      let d = request.resource.data;
      return incidentChange().hasOnly(["location", "updatedAt"])
        && resource.data.status in ["Active", "SupportOnly"]
        && d.location is latlng
        && d.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // The landmark and how precise the fix was. Optional extras, owner only, while the alert is open.
    function validIncidentContextUpdate() {
      let d = request.resource.data;
      return incidentChange().hasOnly(["landmark", "locationAccuracyM", "locationSource", "updatedAt"])
        && resource.data.status in ["Active", "SupportOnly"]
        && validIncidentContext(d)
        && d.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // "Are you safe now?" answered once, after the incident closed. Never required.
    function validResidentFeedbackUpdate() {
      let d = request.resource.data;
      return incidentChange().hasOnly(["residentFeedback", "updatedAt"])
        && resource.data.status in ["Resolved", "Cancelled"]
        && resource.data.get("residentFeedback", null) == null
        && d.residentFeedback is map
        && d.residentFeedback.keys().hasOnly(["safe", "rating"])
        && d.residentFeedback.safe is bool
        && (!("rating" in d.residentFeedback) || (d.residentFeedback.rating is int && d.residentFeedback.rating >= 1 && d.residentFeedback.rating <= 5))
        && d.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // Step 4: the resident closes their own alert. Nothing else may change.
    function validIncidentCancel() {
      let d = request.resource.data;
      return incidentChange().hasOnly(["status", "cancelledAt", "updatedAt"])
        && resource.data.status in ["Active", "SupportOnly"]
        && d.status == "Cancelled"
        && d.cancelledAt is timestamp
        && d.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Users and responders (unchanged except: responders may also report "lastSeenAt" so that
    // dispatch can ignore responders whose location is out of date)
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    function validUsersCreateKeys() {
      return request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly([
        "userId",
        "firstName",
        "lastName",
        "dateOfBirth",
        "gender",
        "phoneNumber",
        "email",
        "role",
        "preferredLanguage",
        "accountStatus",
        "consentCrossBorderTransfer",
        "createdAt",
        "updatedAt",
        "lastLoginAt"
      ]);
    }

    function validUsersUpdateKeys() {
      return request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly([
        "userId",
        "firstName",
        "lastName",
        "dateOfBirth",
        "gender",
        "phoneNumber",
        "email",
        "role",
        "preferredLanguage",
        "accountStatus",
        "consentCrossBorderTransfer",
        "createdAt",
        "updatedAt",
        "lastLoginAt"
      ]);
    }

    function validUsersData(userId) {
      return request.resource.data.userId == userId
        && request.resource.data.firstName is string
        && request.resource.data.lastName is string
        && request.resource.data.dateOfBirth is timestamp
        && request.resource.data.gender is string
        && validPhoneNumber(request.resource.data.phoneNumber)
        && request.resource.data.email is string
        && validPublicRole(request.resource.data.role)
        && validPreferredLanguage(request.resource.data.preferredLanguage)
        && validAccountStatus(request.resource.data.accountStatus)
        && request.resource.data.consentCrossBorderTransfer is bool
        && request.resource.data.createdAt is timestamp
        && request.resource.data.updatedAt is timestamp
        && request.resource.data.lastLoginAt is timestamp;
    }

    function validRespondersCreateKeys() {
      return request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly([
        "responderId",
        "organizationName",
        "organizationType",
        "registrationNumber",
        "verificationStatus",
        "verificationNotes",
        "verifiedBy",
        "verifiedAt",
        "availabilityStatus",
        "currentLatitude",
        "currentLongitude",
        "serviceArea",
        "createdAt",
        "updatedAt"
      ]);
    }

    function validRespondersData(responderId) {
      return request.resource.data.responderId == responderId
        && request.resource.data.organizationName is string
        && validResponderType(request.resource.data.organizationType)
        && request.resource.data.registrationNumber is string
        && validResponderVerificationStatus(request.resource.data.verificationStatus)
        && (request.resource.data.verificationNotes == null || request.resource.data.verificationNotes is string)
        && (request.resource.data.verifiedBy == null || request.resource.data.verifiedBy is string)
        && (request.resource.data.verifiedAt == null || request.resource.data.verifiedAt is timestamp)
        && validAvailabilityStatus(request.resource.data.availabilityStatus)
        && (request.resource.data.currentLatitude == null || request.resource.data.currentLatitude is number)
        && (request.resource.data.currentLongitude == null || request.resource.data.currentLongitude is number)
        && request.resource.data.serviceArea is string
        && request.resource.data.createdAt is timestamp
        && request.resource.data.updatedAt is timestamp;
    }

    // What an administrator may tick for a responder (see functions/dispatch/config.py ALL_CAPABILITIES).
    function validCapabilities(c) {
      return c is list && c.size() <= 7
        && c.hasOnly(["medical_transport", "fire_suppression", "extrication", "law_enforcement", "maternal_care", "gbv_care", "antivenom"]);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Offers: what a responder is asked to do. Written by the dispatch Cloud Function (a minimal
    // briefing, never the resident's full record). The responder may only move the offer forward.
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    function validOfferMove(from, to) {
      return (from == "offered" && to in ["accepted", "declined"])
        || (from == "accepted" && to in ["enroute", "arrived", "completed"])
        || (from == "enroute" && to in ["arrived", "completed"])
        || (from == "arrived" && to == "completed")
        // A unit may stand down from a job it has taken (breakdown, scene unsafe...). Dispatch reconciles and alerts the next unit.
        || (from in ["accepted", "enroute", "arrived"] && to == "withdrawn");
    }

    // Reason a unit gives when it stands down (written with the withdrawal, same update).
    function validWithdrawReason(r) {
      return r in ["cannot_reach", "scene_unsafe", "unit_broken_down", "no_longer_available"];
    }

    // Side-writes a responder may make on their OWN offer, with exact key sets:
    //   seenAt    : the first time the offer screen was shown (once)
    //   transport : the facility chosen after arrival, then its arrival time (once each)
    //   moreUnits : a request for another unit while the responder holds the job (once)
    //   note      : the unit's private notes for this job (any time while holding it)
    function validOfferSideWrite() {
      let d = request.resource.data;
      let c = request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys();
      return (c.hasOnly(["seenAt"])
          && resource.data.status == "offered"
          && resource.data.get("seenAt", null) == null
          && d.seenAt is timestamp)
        || (c.hasOnly(["transport"])
          && resource.data.status == "arrived"
          && (
            // first step: choose the destination
            (resource.data.get("transport", null) == null
              && d.transport.keys().hasOnly(["destinationId", "destinationName", "startedAt", "arrivedAt"])
              && d.transport.destinationId is string
              && d.transport.destinationId.size() > 0
              && exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/facilities/$(d.transport.destinationId))
              && d.transport.destinationName is string
              && d.transport.destinationName.size() <= 120
              && d.transport.startedAt is timestamp
              && (d.transport.get("arrivedAt", null) == null || d.transport.arrivedAt is timestamp))
            // second step: the unit reached that facility; nothing else in the map may change
            || (resource.data.get("transport", null) != null
              && resource.data.transport.get("arrivedAt", null) == null
              && d.transport.keys().hasOnly(["destinationId", "destinationName", "startedAt", "arrivedAt"])
              && d.transport.get("arrivedAt", null) is timestamp
              && d.transport.destinationId == resource.data.transport.destinationId
              && d.transport.destinationName == resource.data.transport.destinationName
              && d.transport.startedAt == resource.data.transport.startedAt)
          ))
        || (c.hasOnly(["moreUnits"])
          && resource.data.status in ["accepted", "enroute", "arrived"]
          && resource.data.get("moreUnits", null) == null
          && d.moreUnits.keys().hasOnly(["role", "count", "note", "requestedAt"])
          && d.moreUnits.role in ["medical_transport", "fire_suppression", "extrication", "law_enforcement", "maternal_care", "gbv_care", "antivenom"]
          && d.moreUnits.count is int
          && d.moreUnits.count >= 1
          && d.moreUnits.count <= 3
          && d.moreUnits.note is string
          && d.moreUnits.note.size() <= 140
          && d.moreUnits.requestedAt == request.time)
        || (c.hasOnly(["note", "noteUpdatedAt"])
          && resource.data.status in ["accepted", "enroute", "arrived", "completed"]
          && d.note is string
          && d.note.size() <= 500
          && d.noteUpdatedAt == request.time);
    }

    // One live position document. Same shape whether a unit or the resident writes it.
    function validTrackPoint() {
      return request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly(["lat", "lng", "heading", "speed", "accuracy", "at"])
        && request.resource.data.lat is number && request.resource.data.lat >= -90 && request.resource.data.lat <= 90
        && request.resource.data.lng is number && request.resource.data.lng >= -180 && request.resource.data.lng <= 180
        && request.resource.data.at is timestamp;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Collections
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
    match /users/{userId} {
      // Owners read their own profile. Administrators keep access to staff and responder accounts only: a resident's
      // name and phone are reachable through the logged reveal_contact callable, never by reading this document.
      allow read: if isOwner(userId) || (isSystemAdmin() && resource.data.get("role", "") != "Resident");

      allow create: if isOwner(userId)
        && !exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/users/$(userId))
        && validUsersCreateKeys()
        && validUsersData(userId)
        && (
          (request.resource.data.role == "Resident" && request.resource.data.accountStatus == "Active")
          ||
          (request.resource.data.role == "Responder" && request.resource.data.accountStatus == "PendingVerification")
        );

      // Owners may edit profile fields, but cannot change their identity, role, or creation time.
      allow update: if (isOwner(userId) || isSystemAdmin())
        && validUsersUpdateKeys()
        && validUsersData(userId)
        && request.resource.data.userId == resource.data.userId
        && request.resource.data.role == resource.data.role
        && request.resource.data.createdAt == resource.data.createdAt;

      allow delete: if false;
    }

    match /responders/{responderId} {
      // Live locations are private: only the responder and administrators can read a responder record.
      allow read: if isOwner(responderId) || isSystemAdmin();

      allow create: if isOwner(responderId)
        && !exists(/databases/$(database)/documents/responders/$(responderId))
        && validRespondersCreateKeys()
        && validRespondersData(responderId)
        && request.resource.data.registrationNumber.size() >= 5
        && request.resource.data.registrationNumber.size() <= 30
        && request.resource.data.verificationStatus == "Pending"
        && request.resource.data.verificationNotes == null
        && request.resource.data.verifiedBy == null
        && request.resource.data.verifiedAt == null
        && request.resource.data.availabilityStatus == "Offline";

      // Owners can change availability, location and the "last seen" time; only admins can change verification fields.
      allow update: if (
          isOwner(responderId)
          && request.resource.data.registrationNumber == resource.data.registrationNumber
          && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
            "availabilityStatus",
            "currentLatitude",
            "currentLongitude",
            "lastSeenAt"
          ])
          && request.resource.data.availabilityStatus in ["Available", "Busy", "Offline"]
          && (request.resource.data.currentLatitude == null || request.resource.data.currentLatitude is number)
          && (request.resource.data.currentLongitude == null || request.resource.data.currentLongitude is number)
          && (request.resource.data.get("lastSeenAt", request.time) == null || request.resource.data.get("lastSeenAt", request.time) is timestamp)
        ) || (
          isSystemAdmin()
          && request.resource.data.registrationNumber == resource.data.registrationNumber
          && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
            "verificationStatus",
            "verificationNotes",
            "verifiedBy",
            "verifiedAt",
            "capabilities"
          ])
          && validCapabilities(request.resource.data.get("capabilities", []))
          && request.resource.data.verificationStatus in ["Pending", "Verified", "Rejected", "Suspended"]
          && (request.resource.data.verificationNotes == null || request.resource.data.verificationNotes is string)
          && (request.resource.data.verifiedBy == null || request.resource.data.verifiedBy is string)
          && (request.resource.data.verifiedAt == null || request.resource.data.verifiedAt is timestamp)
        );

      allow delete: if false;
    }

    match /trustedContacts/{contactId} {
      // Client reads and writes are scoped to the UID stored on the document.
      allow read: if isOwner(resource.data.userId);

      // No per-user contact-count limit is enforced by these rules.
      allow create: if isOwner(request.resource.data.userId)
        && validTrustedContactData()
        && request.resource.data.isActive == true;

      allow update: if isOwner(resource.data.userId)
        && request.resource.data.userId == resource.data.userId
        && request.resource.data.createdAt == resource.data.createdAt
        && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
          "name",
          "phoneNumber",
          "relationship",
          "isActive",
          "updatedAt"
        ])
        && validTrustedContactData();

      // Deactivate contacts instead of deleting their records.
      allow delete: if false;
    }

    match /safetyCheckins/{checkinId} {
      // Client reads and writes are scoped to the UID stored on the document.
      allow read: if isOwner(resource.data.userId);

      allow create: if isOwner(request.resource.data.userId)
        && validSafetyCheckinCreate();

      // Clients can close a check-in but cannot change its owner or timing fields.
      allow update: if isOwner(resource.data.userId)
        && request.resource.data.userId == resource.data.userId
        && request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly([
          "status",
          "updatedAt"
        ])
        && request.resource.data.status in ["CheckedIn", "Cancelled", "Expired"]
        && request.resource.data.updatedAt is timestamp;

      allow delete: if false;
    }

    match /incidents/{incidentId} {
      // A resident sees only their own alerts. Administrators see all of them.
      allow read: if isOwner(resource.data.userId) || isSystemAdmin();

      allow create: if isOwner(request.resource.data.userId)
        && validIncidentCreate();

      allow update: if isOwner(resource.data.userId)
        && request.resource.data.userId == resource.data.userId
        && (
          validIncidentQuickUpdate()
          || validIncidentCompleteUpdate()
          || validIncidentCancel()
          || validIncidentLocationUpdate()
          || validIncidentContextUpdate()
          || validResidentFeedbackUpdate()
        );

      allow delete: if false;
    }

    match /offers/{offerId} {
      // A responder reads only the offers addressed to them. Administrators read all.
      allow read: if isSignedIn()
        && (resource.data.responderId == request.auth.uid || isSystemAdmin());

      // Offers are created and removed only by the dispatch Cloud Function.
      allow create, delete: if false;

      allow update: if isSignedIn()
        && resource.data.responderId == request.auth.uid
        && (
          (request.resource.data.diff(resource.data).affectedKeys().hasOnly(["status", "respondedAt", "updatedAt", "withdrawReason"])
            && validOfferMove(resource.data.status, request.resource.data.status)
            && (request.resource.data.status != "withdrawn" || validWithdrawReason(request.resource.data.withdrawReason))
            && (resource.data.status != "offered" || request.time < resource.data.expiresAt))
          || validOfferSideWrite()
        );
    }

    // ---- Phase 3A: directory, help options, live tracking ----
    // Directory of listed places: any signed-in user can read; only administrators (and the import script, which bypasses rules) can write.
    match /facilities/{facilityId} {
      allow read: if isSignedIn();
      allow write: if isSystemAdmin();
    }

    // The 1-3 help cards for one alert: only the alert's owner and administrators can read them; only Cloud Functions write them.
    match /incidents/{incidentId}/options/{optionId} {
      allow read: if isSignedIn()
        && (isSystemAdmin() || get(/databases/$(database)/documents/incidents/$(incidentId)).data.userId == request.auth.uid);
      allow write: if false;
    }

    // Route cache: Cloud Functions only.
    match /incidents/{incidentId}/routes/{responderId} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }

    // Live positions for one alert. A unit that HOLDS the job shares its position so the resident sees it coming;
    // the resident shares their own position while the alert is open so the unit sees them (Uber-style mutual tracking).
    // The resident of this alert, a unit holding this job, and administrators can read these documents.
    match /incidents/{incidentId}/tracks/{trackOwnerId} {
      allow read: if isSignedIn()
        && (isSystemAdmin() || trackOwnerId == request.auth.uid
            || get(/databases/$(database)/documents/incidents/$(incidentId)).data.userId == request.auth.uid
            || get(/databases/$(database)/documents/offers/$(incidentId + "_" + request.auth.uid)).data.status in ["accepted", "enroute", "arrived"]);

      allow create, update: if isSignedIn() && validTrackPoint()
        && (
          // the unit holding the job
          (trackOwnerId == request.auth.uid
            && get(/databases/$(database)/documents/offers/$(incidentId + "_" + request.auth.uid)).data.status in ["accepted", "enroute", "arrived"])
          ||
          // the resident, only while their own alert is still open
          (trackOwnerId == request.auth.uid
            && get(/databases/$(database)/documents/incidents/$(incidentId)).data.userId == request.auth.uid
            && get(/databases/$(database)/documents/incidents/$(incidentId)).data.status in ["Active", "SupportOnly"])
        );
      allow delete: if false;
    }

    // ---- Phase 4D: the record and alerts for help ----
    // Everything that happened on one alert. Written only by Cloud Functions (the Admin SDK bypasses these rules).
    match /incidents/{incidentId}/events/{eventId} {
      allow read: if isSystemAdmin();
      allow write: if false;
    }

    // Caller contact used for dispatch. Cloud Functions only; clients reach it through reveal_contact (logged).
    match /incidents/{incidentId}/private/{docId} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }

    // A responder's SOS. Administrators see it; no client writes it.
    match /sos_alerts/{sosId} {
      allow read: if isSystemAdmin();
      allow write: if false;
    }

    // Push tokens: a signed-in user stores ONLY their own device token. The dispatch function reads it with the Admin SDK.
    match /deviceTokens/{userId} {
      allow read: if isOwner(userId);
      allow create, update: if isOwner(userId)
        && request.resource.data.keys().hasOnly(["token", "platform", "updatedAt"])
        && request.resource.data.token is string && request.resource.data.token.size() > 20 && request.resource.data.token.size() < 400
        && request.resource.data.platform in ["android", "ios", "web"]
        && request.resource.data.updatedAt is timestamp;
      allow delete: if isOwner(userId);
    }

    // Training messages are written only by the save_text_feedback_fn Cloud Function.
    match /text_feedback/{feedbackId} {
      allow read, write: if false;
    }

    match /auditLogs/{logId} {
      allow read: if isSystemAdmin();
      // Cloud Functions use the Admin SDK; clients must not forge audit history.
      allow write: if false;
    }
  }
}

<<<END>>>

  "fieldOverrides": []

##### FILE: functions\dispatch\notify.py (17 lines)
<<<BEGIN>>>
"""Push notifications to responders (Firebase Cloud Messaging). Sends the MINIMUM: priority, category, distance. Never any resident detail."""
from firebase_admin import messaging


def send_offer_push(db, offer):
    s = db.collection("deviceTokens").document(offer["responderId"]).get()
    token = (s.to_dict() or {}).get("token") if s.exists else None
    if not token:
        return
    label = offer["category"] + (" · " + offer["subtype"].replace("_", " ") if offer.get("subtype") and not offer.get("confidential") else "")
    messaging.send(messaging.Message(
        token=token,
        notification=messaging.Notification(title=f"NEW ALERT · {offer['priority'].upper()} PRIORITY", body=f"{label} · {offer['distanceKm']} km away"),
        data={"type": "offer", "offerId": offer["id"], "incidentId": offer["incidentId"]},
        android=messaging.AndroidConfig(priority="high", ttl=60, notification=messaging.AndroidNotification(channel_id="emergency_offers", sound="default")),
        apns=messaging.APNSConfig(headers={"apns-priority": "10"}, payload=messaging.APNSPayload(aps=messaging.Aps(sound="default")))))

<<<END>>>
